Produces: Infinite colorless mana; Infinite proliferate for permanents
Start: Gilder Bairn (battlefield); Iceberg (battlefield); Utopia Vow (battlefield: attached to Gilder Bairn)

  Repeat from step 2.
  Once you have infinite counters on Iceberg, you may instead double the counters on any other permanents.

Color identity: GU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {2}{U}
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers
Start: Ashaya, Soul of the Wild (battlefield); Blossoming Tortoise (battlefield); Spawning Pit (battlefield: with two or more charge counters on it); Katsumasa, the Animator (battlefield); Doc Samson, Super Psychiatrist (battlefield)
Steps: Animate Spawning Pit using Katsumasa
  Activate Spawning Pit's second ability by removing two charge counters from it due to Blossoming Tortoise, creating a 2/2 Spawn artifact creature token.
  Activate Spawning Pit's first ability by sacrificing the Spawn, putting two charge counters on it.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 654-1309-5531-5694-8110
Cards: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods + Famished Worldsire + Eldrazi Confluence + Amulet of Vigor + Salvager of Secrets
Color identity: GU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); Amulet of Vigor (battlefield); Salvager of Secrets (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Salvager of Secrets and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Salvager of Secrets and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield
  When the lands enter, Amulet of Vigor triggers that many times, untapping the lands.
  Resolve the Salvager of Secrets trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield
  When the land(s) enter, Amulet of Vigor triggers that many times, untapping the land(s)
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Salvager of Secrets and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

## Combo 654-1479-5700-5701-7892
Cards: Kodama of the East Tree + Petrified Field + Echoing Deeps + Amulet of Vigor + Thranduil the Strategist
Color identity: GU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite landfall triggers
Start: Kodama of the East Tree (battlefield); Petrified Field (hand); Echoing Deeps (graveyard); Amulet of Vigor (battlefield); Thranduil the Strategist (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You are able to play a land this turn.
Steps: Play Petrified Field.
  When Petrified Field enters the battlefield, Kodama of the East Tree and Thranduil the Strategist trigger.
  Resolve the Kodama of the East Tree trigger, putting nothing onto the battlefield.
  Holding priority, activate Petrified Field's second ability by tapping and sacrificing it, returning Echoing Deeps from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Thranduil the Strategist trigger, creating a token.
  When the token enters the battlefield, Kodama of the East Tree triggers, putting Echoing Deeps from your hand onto the battlefield as a copy of Petrified Field.
  When Echoing Deeps enters the battlefield, Thranduil the Strategist and Amulet of Vigor trigger.
  Resolve the Amulet of Vigor trigger, untapping Echoing Deeps.
  Holding priority, activate Echoing Deeps using Petrified Field's second ability by tapping and sacrificing it, returning Petrified Field from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Thranduil the Strategist trigger, creating a token.
  When the token enters the battlefield, Kodama of the East Tree triggers, putting Petrified Field from your hand onto the battlefield.
  When Petrified Field enters the battlefield, Thranduil the Strategist triggers.
  Repeat from step 4.

  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield
  When the land(s) enter, Amulet of Vigor triggers that many times, untapping the land(s)
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Scholar of the Ages and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

## Combo 813-5478-5531-5694-8110
Cards: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods + Famished Worldsire + Eldrazi Confluence + Scholar of the Ages + Spelunking
Color identity: GU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0; Return all instant and sorcery cards from your graveyard to your hand
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); Scholar of the Ages (battlefield); Spelunking (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Scholar of the Ages and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Scholar of the Ages and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield untapped due to Spelunking.
  Resolve the Scholar of the Ages trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield untapped due to Spelunking.
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Scholar of the Ages and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.
  An infinite card draw result has been added to this combo due to Cultivator Colossus or a similar card. This likely limits the other results based on the number of cards in your library.

  An opponent cannot block Ascendant Spirit.

  Activate Ascendant Spirit using Devoted Druid's second ability by putting a -1/-1 counter on it, untapping it.
  Repeat three additional times.
  Activate Ascendant Spirit's third ability by paying {S}{S}{S}{S}, putting four +1/+1 counters on it and causing it to gain the listed ability.
  Repeat until the number of instances of Ascendant Spirit's gained ability is equal to your deck size.
  Move to your combat phase, and deal combat damage to an opponent using Ascendant Spirit.
  Ascendant Spirit triggers once for each instance of the gained ability it has, causing you to draw that many cards.

Start: Saheeli's Artistry (hand); Skullwinder (battlefield); Great Whale (hand)
Prerequisites: You control up to seven lands that can tap to produce at least {4}{U}{U}.
Steps: Activate up to seven lands by tapping them, adding at least {4}{U}{U}.
  Cast Saheeli's Artistry by paying {4}{U}{U}, creating a token copy of Great Whale that is an artifact.
  When the Great Whale token enters, it triggers, untapping up to seven lands.
  Cast Skullwinder by paying its mana cost.
  When Skullwinder enters, it triggers, returning Saheeli's Artistry from your graveyard to your hand.
  Activate up to seven lands by tapping them, adding at least {4}{U}{U}.
  Cast Saheeli's Artistry by paying {4}{U}{U}, creating a token copy of Skullwinder and the Great Whale token.
  When the Skullwinder and Great Whale tokens enter, they trigger.
  Resolve the Skullwinder token's trigger, returning Saheeli's Artistry from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Great Whale token's trigger, untapping up to seven lands.
  Repeat from step 6.
Notes: If the seven lands can tap to produce {5}{U}{U} or more, this combo will net infinite mana. So long as you produce an extra {U} each loop, you can eventually stop copying Great Whale and copy other creature(s) or artifact(s) instead. You can also create two copies of Skullwinder instead to return other applicable cards to your hand.
